### Beat 0 — Bug fixes ✓ *(2026-07-09 dev; P0 PROD-VERIFIED 2026-07-10)*

| Feature | Date | Notes |
|---|---|---|
| **False "No longer a member" banner P0 — FIXED + PROD-VERIFIED** | Jul 9–10, 2026 | Root-caused a non-reproducible bug to a race: a deliberate delete/leave and the 30s `checkPresence` watchdog both react to one membership loss; the poll fired the removal notice during the reconciliation gap. Fixed across a four-commit arc — DIAG capture (`c9e330f`, temporary) → v1 `resolvingRef` + name fix (`a43a1ba`, reverts the DIAG) → v2 `deliberateLossRef` raised at the START of the delete/leave handler *before* the destructive RPC, cleared in `finally` (`081c641`, the fix that held). Guard spans the whole deliberate action so no poll lands in the gone-server-side-but-unreconciled window; external removals untouched (regression verified: DH removed DT from Test 101, notice still fired + named right). Also fixed banner name-correctness (stale sticky name → threaded `lostName` from pre-update `myHouseholdsRef`). Verified on dev (Slow-3G create/delete churn), then **PROD-VERIFIED 2026-07-10 — no observed defects; the signup-email-flip gate is now cleared.** `selfDepartureRef`/`markSelfDeparture` scaffolding subsequently removed (`5da1c37`). |
| **Blank-catalog on category-delete** | Jul 9, 2026 | Deleting a filtered category left its `rawName` in `selectedCategories`, collapsing the Layer-2 filter predicate to empty → blank Browse (reload/filter-toggle restored it, confirming client filter-state not data loss). Fix (`4d17c58`, App.js): evict `rawName` from `selectedCategories` on delete (mirrors the `householdCategories` eviction) + self-healing predicate that narrows only by categories that still exist, so no path (incl. a realtime delete from another device) can strand the view. Verified on dev. Client-only. |
| **RUM session-replay masking** | Jul 9, 2026 | Splunk replays came back fully masked despite intent to unmask the grocery UI. `sensitivityRules` used key `type:` where the Splunk recorder reads `rule:` → every rule silently skipped → recorder fell back to its privacy-first mask-everything default. One-word fix ×5 (`f9ae19e`, rum.js); selectors unchanged (unmask `body`, mask `input`/`textarea`, exclude Clerk). Follow-up flagged: `unmask body` exposes all rendered text incl. emails/identifiers in member lists/profile headers — add mask rules once class names are confirmed. |
| **Session replay rendering + unmasked** | Jul 9, 2026 | Chased a blank-replay red herring to ground — capture, ingest (200s), versions, console all healthy; the blank frames were **render latency, not a bug.** Replay is now a working diagnostic instrument (it surfaced the blank-catalog bug this session). |

| Feature | Date | Notes |
|---|---|---|
| **Join-activation — REOPEN prod-verified** | Jul 7, 2026 | The dev-green `pendingJoinId` fix (`1c5a916`) failed on prod: an existing user active in "My Household" who pasted an invite never moved — `just_joined_household_id` stayed set, lens never wrote (membership-propagation latency; the interrupted-window caveat became a confirmed prod failure). Root cause: flag consumption + `pendingJoinId` derivation were wired *inside* the banner effect, gated on `household.name !== "My Household"` — exactly false for that user. Fix (`c1ceab2`, App.js): banner effect is display-only; a durable/retriable effect derives the switch from the surviving sessionStorage flag on every relevant render, re-fires `switchHousehold` on each `myHouseholds`/`activeHouseholdId` change, clears the flag ONLY after `activeHouseholdId === joinedId`, and bounded-retries `refreshHouseholds` for late membership. Lens stays single writer. Verified on prod (existing user in prior household → auto-switch → banner → survives hard-reload). See `docs/specs/built/SPEC_join_activates_household_ADDENDUM_reopen.md`. Client-only. |
| **Sign-in/sign-up cold-load fix** | Jul 7, 2026 | On a cold load the Clerk `SignInButton`/`SignUpButton` modal handlers aren't wired until `isLoaded`, but the buttons rendered enabled → a click before load was a dead no-op requiring a refresh (surfaced by a fresh deploy's cold JS cache + a laptop waking from sleep). Fix (`2249d0b`, App.js): gate on the already-imported `isLoaded` — while `!isLoaded` render disabled placeholder buttons (identical dimensions, only cursor+opacity differ → no layout shift), swap to live once loaded. Verified disable-then-trigger on Slow 4G. Client-only. |
| **Per-household staples — `household_staples` (Bug A)** | Jul 7, 2026 | `is_staple` was a single boolean on the shared `is_global=true` row (`household_id=NULL`) → tapping Staple on a global item silently no-op'd (the `catalog_items` UPDATE policy admits only `household_id in (memberships)`; `NULL in (...)` is never true → 0 rows, no error → reverted on the 20s poll), and the Staples filter read that shared flag. Fix (`949a8e9` + migration 016): new `household_staples(household_id, catalog_item_id)` join table, row-presence = stapled, single source of truth for global AND custom; RLS select/insert/delete on `is_member_of`; `catalog_item_id` `ON DELETE CASCADE`; backfilled existing custom staples (dev 1=1, prod 5=5); `get_list_items_for_household` repointed to derive `is_staple` via EXISTS; `toggleStaple`/catalog reads rewritten. Applied to dev + prod. Global staples now persist per-household with no cross-household leak. `catalog_items.is_staple` dormant. |
| **Cold-start empty-Browse race (Bug B)** | Jul 7, 2026 | A first-time user could land on an empty Browse until they tapped a filter (filters only narrow — the data was simply missing). Root: on a cold start the global-catalog read could return empty/errored before session/RLS warmed, then proceed — stranding `catalogMap={}` with `loading` already false and (on the error path) no poll armed. Fix (`7d1e3c2`, useProvisions): the ~50-row global seed set always exists, so an empty result is never legitimate → bounded retry (4 attempts, short backoff) that breaks as soon as rows arrive; zero delay on the normal path. Independent of the staple architecture. Client-only. |
| **New-user cold-start walk — PARTIAL GREEN** | Jul 7, 2026 | Ran the timed zero-state walk on prod with real data. Staples (Bug A) and empty-Browse (Bug B) both fixed and prod-verified. Gate result: PARTIAL GREEN — the false "No longer a member" banner leaked once during staple churn → signup email held on the personal variant (see NOW P0 blocker). |

---

## Strategic Bets

- **The Harbor is the moat.** Switching away from OurProvisions is one thing. Leaving the Harbor means losing shared family history across every app. That's a much harder thing to walk away from.
- **Prices are infrastructure.** The receipt scanner is the intelligence layer. Everything — cross-store comparison, waste detection, savings nudges — flows from it.
- **Digital receipts as distribution.** Grocery chains pivoting to digital receipts represent a partnership and scaling opportunity, not just a data source.
- **Capture signals silently.** Check-off sequence, GPS, timing — captured in the background. No user configuration needed.
- **Provision cycles not just sessions.** The planning unit is a cycle (Plan → Shop → Review → repeat). Sessions are how it gets fulfilled. This model handles partial shops, multi-person splits, and impromptu runs cleanly.
- **The shared list is sacred.** No per-user view preference (hide, filter) ever suppresses what the household has collectively decided to buy. Personalization lives in the view layer; the list is shared truth. *(Added June 8 — the principle behind the per-user-hide fix.)*
